-----------------------------------------------------------------------------








文字快照 :http://www.icrt.com.tw/info_details.php?mlevel1=6&mlevel2=12&news_id=117387&reUrlAddr=L25ld3NfZGF0YWJhc2UucGhwPyZtbGV2ZWwxPTYmbWxldmVsMj0zNw==




... around Taiwan.

The Taiwanese humpback dolphin is a subspecies of the Indo-Pacific humpback dolphin and is found in shallow waters off the western coast of Taiwan throughout the year.

The dolphin is dubbed the "Matsu fish" in Taiwan because it is often spotted around the birthday of sea goddess Matsu in the spring when the ocean is calm.
